## Technical result versus human disposition

| Field | Meaning | Failure effect |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Technical `passed` | Objective-specific, hashed evidence passed at the exact revision | Green technical result |
| Technical `not_tested` | Candidate evidence exists but no objective-specific result was supplied | Not green; not automatically failed |
| Human `pending` | Organization or assessor reconciliation has not occurred | No effect on the technical gate; finding remains `not_assessed` |
| Mandatory prerequisite pending | Licensing, legal, security, or explicit activation approval is required first | Protected function remains fail closed |

**Technical pass criteria:** Every applicable objective has an objective-specific passed or valid not-applicable result, the result artifact is SHA-256 hashed, and the result is bound to the exact release revision.

**Technical fail criteria:** At least one objective-specific exact-revision technical result is failed.

**Human completion criteria:** An authorized organization reviewer or assessor completes the applicable review and records a final signed disposition artifact within the authorized scope.

**Separation invariant:** Organization review or assessor determination may remain pending without converting a passed technical result to failed. The assessment finding remains not_assessed until the authorized human disposition exists.

## Scope and claim boundary

Excluded workstreams: Applications product workstream.

Excluded paths: `app/apps/`, `app/api/apps/`, `app/portal/applications/`, `lib/apps/`, `.github/workflows/application-production-pipeline.yml`, `.github/workflows/application-release-validation.yml`, `scripts/application-market-brain.mjs`, `scripts/application-production-worker.mjs`, `scripts/application-worker-plan.mjs`, `scripts/generate-app-release-bundle.mjs`, `scripts/sync-final-apps.mjs`, `scripts/validate-application-staging.mjs`, `scripts/watch-final-apps.ps1`.

- Permitted: This register records engineering alignment, evidence provenance, artifact integrity, control-objective traceability, and readiness gaps for the named systems and exact source revision.
- Permitted: A final release artifact may be eligible to support an assessment finding only when every fail-closed eligibility condition in this register is satisfied.
- Prohibited: This register does not claim CMMC certification, a CMMC Level 2 status, a MET assessor determination, authorization to process CUI, FedRAMP authorization, FDACS approval, or provider compliance beyond retained evidence.
- Prohibited: Revision 3 engineering alignment does not replace the Revision 2 and June 2018 baseline incorporated by 32 CFR Part 170.
